Glueckstal Deaths, 1833-1885 (H. Ehrman)

        Published by the Odessa Digital Library - 31 Oct 1996
             http://www.odessa3.org

This file contains death records from the villages of Bergdorf, Cassel, 
Glueckstal and Neudorf in South Russia.  The time period covered is from 1833 to 
1885.  Not all years are included. This information was compiled by Harold 
Ehrman and coworkers October 1994 thru October 1996 from the St. Petersburg 
Lutheran Evangelical archives published by LDS.  Contributors to this database 
are Arnold Baier, John Clausing, Harold Ehrman, Joy Fisher, Hiller Goehring, 
Margaret Freeman, Emma Job, Twilamae Johnson, Dorothea Kerr, Loyd Ketterling, 
Billie Lary, Gary Maier, Roy Merkel, Sandy Montana, Gwen Pritzkau, Penny Raile, 
Erwin Rall, Dennis Roth, Richard Sandmeier, Edgar Schulz, Marilyn Simpson, Janet 
Stangl, Tom Stangl, Jeanie White and Cecelia Wolff.  This work may be freely 
copied for nonprofit purposes. revised: 30 October 1996.

Compiled by:

Harold M. Ehrman
1267 Palisades Drive
Pacific Palisades, CA 90272
